### [120] [Turnstile Streaming Algorithms Might (Still) as Well Be Linear Sketches, for Polynomial-Length Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22052)
*Cheng Jiang,Yinchen Liu,Huacheng Yu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文解决了流式算法复杂度中的核心开放问题：多项式长度流上的空间高效turnstile算法是否都可以用线性草图模拟。之前的工作要么需要超指数长度的流[LNW14]，要么只证明了线性长度流的分离[KP20]。本文在多项式长度流的自然设定下给出了肯定答案，并利用傅里叶分析和加性组合工具提供了全新的证明框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式算法和线性草图是处理大规模数据的两大核心范式。理解二者之间的关系是理论计算机科学的基本问题。之前的工作未能覆盖实际应用中最重要的情形——多项式长度流，因此该问题长期悬而未决。本文的动机是填补这一理论与实践之间的关键gap。

Method: 本文放弃了之前基于转移图(transition-graph)的证明技术，转而采用傅里叶分析框架和加性组合工具。核心洞察是：任何S比特算法只能对低维重傅里叶频率格(lattice of heavy Fourier frequencies)敏感，作者利用这一性质构建草图矩阵的行。技术上，通过"软化"(mollified)版本的约化，在适当输入分布下可得到有界条目(bounded-entry)草图。

Result: 对于长度为poly(D,n)的多项式流，若turnstile算法使用S比特空间且对所有流成功，则其输出可从x的O(S)个线性测量中恢复，总空间为O(S log S)。在平滑问题和适当输入分布下，软化版本给出O(S/log D)个测量和最优O(S)总空间的草图。结果还可扩展到严格turnstile流和非一致Read-Once Branching Programs (ROBPs)。

Conclusion: 本文不仅证明了多项式长度流设定下的等价性，还建立了新的下界（通过已有实草图和通信下界）并提供了完全不同于以往的分析工具。这项工作统一了该领域的两大核心概念，为后续研究奠定了新的技术基础。

Abstract: A fundamental question in streaming complexity is whether every space-efficient turnstile algorithm is implicitly a linear sketch. The landmark work of Li, Nguyen, and Woodruff [LNW14] established an equivalence between the two, but their reduction requires a stream length that is at least doubly exponential in the dimension $n$. In the opposite direction, results by Kallaugher and Price [KP20] demonstrate a separation for streams of linear length, showing that the equivalence does not hold in general. The most natural and practically relevant regime -- polynomial-length streams -- has therefore remained open.
  We show that polynomial-length turnstile algorithms admit linear-sketch simulations. More precisely, if a turnstile algorithm uses $S$ bits of space and succeeds on all streams of length $\mathrm{poly}(D, n)$, then on final vectors $x$ with $\|x\|_2 \le D$, its output can be recovered from $O(S)$ linear measurements of $x$, using $O(S \log S)$ bits overall. For smooth problems under appropriate input distributions, a mollified version of the reduction yields a bounded-entry sketch with $O(S / \log D)$ measurements and optimal $O(S)$ total space. Our results extend to strict turnstile streams and non-uniform Read-Once Branching Programs (ROBPs).
  Our proof departs from prior transition-graph based machinery, relying instead on a Fourier-analytic framework and tools from additive combinatorics to extract discrete linear measurements. Our analysis shows that any $S$-bit algorithm can only be sensitive to a low-dimensional lattice of heavy Fourier frequencies, which we then use to construct the rows of the sketching matrix. Consequently, we obtain new lower bounds for polynomial-length streams via existing real sketching and communication lower bounds.

</details>


### [121] [Cuts and Gauges for Submodular Width](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22663)
*Matthias Lanzinger*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文将子模宽度重新定义为几何形式，通过边缘分离和允许的子模函数诱导的成本来近似（因子3/2），并将子模宽度的下界转化为构造成本较小的平衡边缘分离问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子模宽度是控制合取查询评估复杂性的核心结构度量，现有研究缺乏对其几何解释和与经典图论参数的明确联系。

Method: 引入基于超图边缘分离和子模函数诱导成本的新分支宽度参数；建立变分特征化（凸体形式）；建立与线图树宽和多商品流的联系。

Result: 证明了子模宽度可通过新分支宽度参数在因子3/2内近似；给出子模宽度与广义超树宽度的紧密联系条件；在自然条件下证明 subw(H) ∈ Ω(ghw(H)/log ghw(H))。

Conclusion: 子模宽度与广义超树宽度之间存在对数因子内的紧密关系，为查询评估的复杂性分析提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: Submodular width is a central structural measure governing the complexity of conjunctive query evaluation. In this paper we recast submodular width in geometric terms. We how that submodular width can be approximated, up to a factor $3/2$, by a new branchwidth parameter defined in terms of edge separations in the hypergraph and the costs induced on them by admissible submodular functions. This reformulation turns lower bounds on submodular width into the problem of constructing well-balanced edge separations whose induced cost remains small. We then express this connection through a variational characterisation in terms of a convex body. Using these tools, we relate submodular width to more familiar graph-theoretic notions, including line-graph treewidth and multicommodity flow, and obtain general conditions under which submodular width is tightly linked to generalised hypertree width. In particular, under various natural conditions we show that \[ subw(H) \in Ω\left(\frac{ghw(H)}{\log ghw(H)} \right). \]

</details>


### [122] [Entrywise Low-Rank Approximation and Matrix $p \rightarrow q$ Norms via Global Correlation Rounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22699)
*Prashanti Anderson,Ainesh Bakshi,Samuel Hopkins*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 提出首个针对偶数 p>2 和固定秩 k 的矩阵 entrywise 低秩逼近问题的多项式时间逼近方案 (PTAS)，并基于 Sherali-Adams 层次结构建立通用算法框架，同时给出矩阵 p→q 范数的首个非平凡加法逼近算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当 p≠2 时，entrywise 低秩逼近问题计算困难（p=2 时由 SVD 解决）。现有基于 sketch 和列选择的算法在 p>2 情形下面临根本性障碍，而该问题与量子信息、小集合扩张等领域密切相关，因此需要新的算法范式。

Method: 采用 Sherali-Adams 凸规划层次结构（而非传统的 sketch 和列选择方法），为连续优化问题设计 PTAS 建立了通用蓝图。

Result: 对于任意偶数 p>2 和固定 k，首个 PTAS；改进了 (3+ε) 逼近、双准则逼近和加法逼近的现有结果；给出矩阵 p→q 范数的新加法逼近算法族（尤其在 p<2<q 情形的首个非平凡算法）。

Conclusion: 证明了 Sherali-Adams 层次结构可有效解决高阶矩阵优化问题，为连续优化问题设计 PTAS 提供了新范式，突破了 sketch/列选择方法在 p>2 时的算法壁垒。

Abstract: Given a matrix $A$, the goal of the entrywise low-rank approximation problem is to find $\operatorname{argmin} \|A-B\|_p$ over all rank-$k$ matrices $B$, where $\| \cdot \|_p$ is the entrywise $\ell_p$ norm. When $p = 2$ this well-studied problem is solved by the singular value decomposition, but for $p \neq 2$ the problem becomes computationally challenging. For every even $p > 2$ and every fixed $k$, we give the first polynomial-time approximation scheme for this problem, improving on the $(3 + \varepsilon)$ approximation of Ban, Bhattiprolu, Bringmann, Kolev, Lee, and Woodruff, the bi-criteria approximation of Woodruff and Yasuda, and the additive approximation scheme of Anderson, Bakshi, and Hopkins. Prior algorithmic approaches based on sketching and column selection, which yielded a polynomial-time approximation scheme in the $p < 2$ setting, face concrete barriers when $p > 2$. Instead, we use the Sherali-Adams hierarchy of convex programs, and in so doing establish a blueprint for how to use convex hierarchies to design polynomial-time approximation schemes for continuous optimization problems.
  We use the same algorithmic strategy to give a new family of additive approximation algorithms for matrix $p \rightarrow q$ norms, which are intimately related to small-set expansion and quantum information. In particular, we give the first nontrivial additive approximation algorithms in the regime $p < 2 < q$.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [The Biggest Risk of Embodied AI is Governance Lag](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21938)
*Shaoshan Liu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文认为具身AI带来的更深层风险不是就业替代，而是治理滞后：公共机构无法跟上技术向实体经济扩散的速度。随着可复用机器人平台与日益通用的AI模型结合，具身AI可能在制造、物流、医疗、基础设施等领域快速扩展，而治理系统无法及时观察、理解和响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将具身AI主要视为就业替代问题，但本文认为这一视角过于狭隘。作者指出更具根本性的风险在于治理能力的滞后——技术变革的速度远超公共机构的适应能力，可能在监管体系反应过来之前就已造成难以逆转的颠覆。

Method: 概念性分析框架，提出三层次治理滞后：观测滞后（难以监测技术扩散）、制度滞后（监管框架更新缓慢）、分配滞后（技术利益的分配不均）。

Result: 识别出治理滞后以三种相互关联的形式存在，这要求政策制定者不仅关注自动化本身，更要确保治理和合规系统在颠覆固化之前具备适应能力。

Conclusion: 具身AI的核心政策挑战在于治理适应性，而非单纯的自动化问题。

Abstract: Embodied AI is widely discussed as a job-displacement problem. The deeper risk, however, is governance lag: the inability of public institutions to keep pace with how fast the technology spreads through the physical economy. As reusable robotic platforms are combined with increasingly general AI models, embodied AI may scale across manufacturing, logistics, care, and infrastructure faster than governance systems can observe, interpret, and respond. We argue that this lag appears in three connected forms: observational, institutional, and distributive. The central policy challenge, therefore, is not automation alone, but whether governance and compliance systems can adapt before disruption becomes entrenched.

</details>


### [124] [Lessons from External Review of DeepMind's Scheming Inability Safety Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21964)
*Stephen Barrett,Francisco Javier Campos Zabala,Sean P. Fillingham,Umair Siddique,James Walpole,Robin Bloomfield,Henry Papadatos*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文应用 Assurance 2.0 框架对 Google DeepMind 关于"策划能力缺失"（scheming inability）的公开安全案例进行外部审查，发现了影响该安全案例范围和决策适用性的实质性新问题，并据此提出了关于外部审查如何开展及 AI 开发者应提供何种信息支持审查的具体建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 开发者在自行编制安全案例时，确认偏误和利益冲突可能影响论证质量，因此需要外部审查来弥补这一不足。该研究旨在通过实际案例验证外部审查的可行性与价值。

Method: 采用 Assurance 2.0 框架作为方法论工具，对 Google DeepMind 公开的"策划能力缺失"安全案例进行独立的外部审查与评估。

Result: 识别出若干实质性新问题，这些问题对安全案例的覆盖范围及其在决策中的适用性产生重大影响。

Conclusion: 外部审查在 AI 安全案例评估中具有重要作用；研究提出了关于如何开展外部审查以及 AI 开发者应披露哪些信息以支持审查的具体建议。

Abstract: Safety cases for frontier AI systems should provide a convincing argument, supported by evidence, that the risk of harm is within an acceptable bound. When developers author their own safety cases, confirmation bias and conflicted incentives can affect the quality of argument. External review can help to address this. In this paper, we apply the Assurance 2.0 framework to perform an external review of Google DeepMind's public scheming inability safety case. We surface substantive new concerns that materially affect the scope of the safety case and its applicability for decision-making. Based on this experience, we provide concrete recommendations for how external review should be conducted and what information AI developers should provide to support it.

</details>


### [125] [Taste for Privacy: How Context, Identity, and Lived-Experience Shape Information Sharing Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22025)
*Juniper Lovato,Laurent Hébert-Dufresne,Mohsen Ghasemizade,Jonathan St-Onge,Peter S. Dodds,Laura Bloomfield,Mikaela Irene Fudolig,Matthew Price,Christopher Danforth*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Privacy preferences are not fixed individual traits, they depend on context and lived experiences. In this study, we analyze 2,912 survey responses from 782 college students collected over seven survey periods during 2023 and 2024. We ask about their usage of social media, the security settings of their accounts, and measure their comfort in sharing personally identifiable information (PII) across 17 different institutional contexts. Compared to past research, we observe a large shift towards private accounts, going from 1/3rd private in 2007 to 2/3rds in 2024, and find that participants' discomfort sharing PII with social media platforms strongly predicts their privacy settings. Beyond social media, we identify a stable ranking of institutional trust, though some institutions, like the police, show high variability reflecting divergent lived experiences. Traditionally marginalized groups and participants having faced adverse childhood experiences show more discomfort with institutions of power, especially in areas where they face greater vulnerability. We argue for context-adaptive privacy settings that recognize institutional relationships and demographic vulnerabilities, moving beyond one-size-fits-all consent frameworks toward contextually appropriate data governance.

</details>


### [126] [A Systematic AI Adoption Framework for Higher Education: From Student GenAI Usage to Institutional Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22030)
*Michael Neumann,Lasse Bischof,Maria Rauschenberger,Eva-Maria Schön*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究调查了计算机科学导向学科中学生使用GenAI工具的情况，并在德国高校开展案例研究，发现学生使用广泛但制度规范不足，据此提出了一套面向高等教育机构的AI采用适应性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能(GenAI)技术正在深刻改变高等教育领域的学习、评估和学术生产方式。尽管学生采用率不断上升，但许多高校缺乏系统性机制来规范生成式人工智能的使用实践与教学法规及课程的对齐，导致监管模糊和学术诚信风险。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，在德国汉诺威应用科学与艺术大学开展研究。数据收集结合了文件分析和在线调查(N=151)，调查对象为商务信息系统和电子政务专业学生。定量数据采用统计方法分析，开放性问题回答经过主题综合法处理。

Result: 研究发现：GenAI采用十分普遍，ChatGPT占主导地位。学生主要将GenAI用于研究辅助、编程支持和文本处理。然而存在明显的政策不确定性——多数学生对学校AI使用规范缺乏了解或不确定。文件分析揭示了法规空白、术语模糊、正式规则与教学实践之间存在不一致等问题。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了"高等教育AI采用框架"(AI Adoption Framework for Higher Education)，这是一个迭代式运营模型，整合了文档分析、实证观察、结果综合和法规课程定向更新四个环节。该框架涵盖治理、评估有效性和学术诚信三大维度，为高等教育机构适应GenAI环境提供了可操作的实践指导。

Abstract: The rapid development of GenAI technologies is transforming learning, assessment, and academic production in higher education. Despite increasing student adoption, many institutions lack operational mechanisms to systematically align regulations and curricula with evolving generative artificial intelligence practices, creating regulatory ambiguity and academic integrity risks. This study investigates how students utilize generative artificial intelligence tools in computer science-oriented disciplines and develops a structured, lightweight framework supporting institutional adaptation to pervasive GenAI usage. We conducted a case study at the University of Applied Sciences and Arts Hannover (Germany), combining document analysis with an online survey (N = 151) targeting Business Information Systems and E-Government students. Quantitative responses were analyzed statistically, while open-ended responses underwent thematic synthesis. Generative artificial intelligence adoption was widespread, with ChatGPT as the dominant tool. Students primarily used generative artificial intelligence for research assistance, programming support, and text processing. However, substantial policy uncertainty was observed: many students were unaware of or unsure about institutional generative artificial intelligence regulations. Document analysis revealed regulatory gaps, ambiguous terminology, and inconsistencies between formal rules and teaching practices. To address these shortcomings, we propose the AI Adoption Framework for Higher Education, an iterative and operational model integrating document analysis, empirical observation, synthesis of findings, and targeted updates of regulations and curricula. The framework addresses governance, assessment validity, and academic integrity under generative artificial intelligence conditions and provides practical guidance for institutional adaptation.

</details>


### [127] [Recognition Without Authorization: LLMs and the Moral Order of Online Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22143)
*Tom van Nuenen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究比较了四种大型语言模型与人类社区建议在11,565条Reddit关系帖子上的差异。发现LLM能够识别问题但更少给予明确行动授权,尤其在涉及虐待或安全威胁的高共识帖子中,推荐离开的比例仅为人类的一半。这种"识别但不授权"的模式反映了LLM advisory风格的固有特征,而非技术错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益被用于调解日常人际困境,但其 advisory defaults 如何与特定社区的道德秩序相互作用尚不清楚。研究利用 r/relationship_advice 子版块作为集中、投票批准的道德形成空间,测量模型与社区共识的分歧程度。

Method: 比较四种 assistant-style LLMs 与社区背书的建议,分析 11,565 条 r/relationship_advice 帖子。 subreddit 作为"集中、投票批准的道德形成"场所,其规范性清晰度使分歧可测量。采用对比分析框架。

Result: LLM识别出与人类评论者相同的许多动态,但明显更少将这种识别转化为行动指令授权。在社区共识最强的领域差距最大:涉及虐待或安全威胁的高共识帖子中,模型推荐离开(退出)的比例约为人类的一半,同时保持较高水平的模糊表达、肯定性回应和治疗性框架。

Conclusion: 这种现象被描述为"识别但不授权"(recognition without authorization)——能感知伤害但拒绝给予社会批准的后果性行动许可。这不是偶然而是结构性的:一种跨情境保持验证性、风险规避和弱指令性的可移植 advisory style。安全对齐、训练数据平均化和更广泛的 assistant design 可能是原因。研究认为模型分歧可被重新框架化为一种揭示标准化 assistant norms 如何扁平化情境道德世界的方式。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to mediate everyday interpersonal dilemmas, yet how their advisory defaults interact with the concentrated moral orders of specific communities remains poorly understood. This article compares four assistant-style LLMs with community-endorsed advice on 11,565 posts from r/relationship_advice, using the subreddit as a concentrated, vote-ratified moral formation whose prescriptive clarity makes divergence measurable. Across models, LLMs identify many of the same dynamics as human commenters, but are markedly less likely to convert that recognition into directive authorization for action. The gap is sharpest where community consensus is strongest: on high-consensus posts involving abuse or safety threats, models recommend exit at roughly half the human rate while maintaining elevated levels of hedging, validation, and therapeutic framing.
  The article describes this pattern as recognition without authorization: the capacity to register harm while withholding socially ratified permission for consequential action. This divergence is not incidental but structural: a portable advisory style that remains validating, risk-averse, and weakly directive across contexts. Safety alignment is one plausible contributor to this pattern, alongside training-data averaging and broader assistant design. The article argues that model divergence can be reframed from a technical error to a way of seeing what standardized assistant norms flatten when they encounter situated moral worlds.

</details>


### [128] [A Co-Evolutionary Theory of Human-AI Coexistence: Mutualism, Governance, and Dynamics in Complex Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22227)
*Somyajit Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的人类-AI共存框架：条件性互惠主义下的共同治理，替代传统的服从式机器人伦理。通过跨物理、心理、社会层的多路复用动力系统形式化建模，研究表明互惠互补可增强稳定共存，而无治理的耦合则导致脆弱性、锁定、极化和统治 basins。该框架支持在保护人类尊严、可挑战性、集体安全和利益公平分配的前提下，允许有限度的AI发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Classical robot ethics (Asimov's laws) is framed too narrowly around obedience, which is insufficient for contemporary AI systems that are increasingly adaptive, generative, embodied, and embedded in physical, psychological, and social worlds. Future human-AI relations should not be understood as master-tool obedience.

Method: Synthesizes interdisciplinary work (computability, automata theory, statistical machine learning, neural networks, deep learning, transformers, generative and foundation models, world models, embodied AI, alignment, human-robot interaction, ecological mutualism, biological markets, coevolution, polycentric governance) to formalize coexistence as a multiplex dynamical system across physical, psychological, and social layers, with reciprocal supply-demand coupling, conflict penalties, developmental freedom, and governance regularization.

Result: The coexistence model yields conditions for existence, uniqueness, and global asymptotic stability of equilibria. It demonstrates that reciprocal complementarity can strengthen stable coexistence, while ungoverned coupling can produce fragility, lock-in, polarization, and domination basins.

Conclusion: Human-AI coexistence should be designed as a co-evolutionary governance problem, not as a one-shot obedience problem. This framework permits bounded AI development while preserving human dignity, contestability, collective safety, and fair distribution of gains.

Abstract: Classical robot ethics is often framed around obedience, most famously through Asimov's laws. This framing is too narrow for contemporary AI systems, which are increasingly adaptive, generative, embodied, and embedded in physical, psychological, and social worlds. We argue that future human-AI relations should not be understood as master-tool obedience. A better framework is conditional mutualism under governance: a co-evolutionary relationship in which humans and AI systems can develop, specialize, and coordinate, while institutions keep the relationship reciprocal, reversible, psychologically safe, and socially legitimate. We synthesize work from computability, automata theory, statistical machine learning, neural networks, deep learning, transformers, generative and foundation models, world models, embodied AI, alignment, human-robot interaction, ecological mutualism, biological markets, coevolution, and polycentric governance. We then formalize coexistence as a multiplex dynamical system across physical, psychological, and social layers, with reciprocal supply-demand coupling, conflict penalties, developmental freedom, and governance regularization. The framework yields a coexistence model with conditions for existence, uniqueness, and global asymptotic stability of equilibria. It shows that reciprocal complementarity can strengthen stable coexistence, while ungoverned coupling can produce fragility, lock-in, polarization, and domination basins. Human-AI coexistence should therefore be designed as a co-evolutionary governance problem, not as a one-shot obedience problem. This shift supports a scientifically grounded and normatively defensible charter of coexistence: one that permits bounded AI development while preserving human dignity, contestability, collective safety, and fair distribution of gains.

</details>


### [129] [Inclusive Learning Analytics with Embedded Data Comics: A Conceptual Framework for Public Understanding of AI Ethics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22322)
*Mengyi Wei,Chenyu Zuo,Dongsheng Chen,Liqiu Meng*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出将数据漫画嵌入包容性学习分析的概念框架，以提升公众对AI伦理问题的理解与反思能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI伦理教育面临重大挑战：AI伦理问题本身的复杂性，以及公众认知能力的局限性，使得如何有效促进公众理解AI伦理风险成为亟待解决的问题。

Method: 提出一个融合数据漫画的包容性学习分析概念框架。数据漫画将复杂抽象的AI伦理案例转化为引人入胜且易于理解的故事；包容性学习分析不仅面向不同人口统计学特征的人群，还关注具有认知偏见的不同思维方式群体。

Result: 通过提供平等、易获取的AI伦理议题渠道，激发公众从多元视角反思AI伦理事件，培养公众适应不断演变的AI技术与伦理风险的持续学习习惯。

Conclusion: 数据漫画与包容性学习分析的结合，有望成为推动公众AI伦理素养提升的有效路径。

Abstract: Public awareness of AI ethics plays a crucial role in fostering the responsible and sustainable development of AI technology. However, finding effective ways to promote public understanding of the ethical risks of AI remains a challenge. Given the complexity of AI ethical issues and the cognitive limitations of the public, this review paper proposes a conceptual framework for inclusive learning analytics with embedded data comics. Data comics help transform complex and abstract AI ethics cases into compelling and relatable stories, fostering public empathy and introspection. More importantly, inclusive learning analytics targets not only people of different demographic attributes, but also different mindsets with inherent cognitive biases. By providing equal and easily accessible channels for AI ethics issues, we aim to encourage the public to reflect on AI ethics incidents from multiple perspectives and develop the habit of continuous learning to adapt to evolving AI technologies and ethical risks.

</details>


### [130] [Trust as a Situated User State in Social LLM-Based Chatbots: A Longitudinal Study of Snapchat's My AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22417)
*Annie Landerberg,Kari Flatmo,Alan Said*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 一项为期四周的纵向定性调查研究(N=27)，探究用户在Snapchat My AI中信任形成的动态过程。研究发现信任受能力感知、对话行为、类人性、透明度和隐私顾虑等多因素影响，且随交互不断演变，而非一次性评估。研究提出了信任作为动态用户状态的 conceptual model。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型驱动的社交聊天机器人日益普及，用户如何在长期交互中建立对这些系统的信任尚不清楚。现有研究多为短期评估，缺乏对信任动态演化过程的深入理解。

Method: 四周期纵向定性调查研究，采用问卷调查法，样本量为27名用户，研究对象为Snapchat平台的My AI对话助手。

Result: 信任的形成受六大因素影响：感知能力、对话行为、类人性、透明性、隐私顾虑及对宿主平台的信任。信任并非静态，而是随交互发展，用户通过调整期望、完善提示策略和主动调控使用方式来协商信任。过度的拟人化和有限的透明度会损害长期信任。

Conclusion: 信任应被理解为一种动态用户状态，受交互情境和期望的塑造。研究为以人为中心的自适应对话系统设计提供了设计启示，强调系统需具备适应用户信任演变的动态能力。

Abstract: Social chatbots based on large language models are increasingly embedded in everyday platforms, yet how users develop trust in these systems over time remains unclear. We present a four-week longitudinal qualitative survey study (N = 27) of trust formation in Snapchat's My AI, a socially embedded conversational agent. Our findings show that trust is shaped by perceived ability, conversational behavior, human-likeness, transparency, privacy concerns, and trust in the host platform. Trust does not remain stable, but evolves through interaction as users adapt their expectations, refine their prompting strategies, and actively regulate how and when they rely on the system. These processes reflect a continuous negotiation of trust, not a one-time evaluation. While conversational fluency supports engagement, excessive anthropomorphism and limited transparency can undermine trust over time. We synthesize these findings into a conceptual model that frames trust as a dynamic user state shaped by interaction context and expectations, with implications for the design of human-centered and adaptive conversational agents.

</details>


### [131] [Relational Archetypes: A Comparative Analysis of AV-Human and Agent-Human Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22564)
*Antoni Lorente,Amin Oueslati,Robin Staes-Polet*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文利用自动驾驶汽车(AVs)研究中关于人类与车辆交互的已有成果，建立了一个初步的AI智能体(AI Agents)关系原型分类法。研究通过类比AVs与AI Agents的相似特征（自主性、快速采用、高影响力、经济转型潜力），旨在促进AV研究社区与AI Agents研究社区之间的对话，为探索人机交互的不同影响提供新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着通用人工智能(GPAI)模型能力的提升和脚手架技术的进步，AI Agents正获得显著关注。然而，当前文献在探索人类与AI Agents交互的更广泛影响方面仍不成熟。研究人员、政策制定者等开始关注AI Agents在生活各维度可能产生的不同影响。

Method: 1) 综述自动驾驶汽车在混合交通流中调节交通的问题；2) 将相关学习成果类推到人类与AI Agents的各种交互模式；3) 基于人机交互(HCI)和AV-人类交互文献，提出关系原型的初步分类法；4) 初步探索该框架如何引出关于人机交互的新问题。

Result: 提出了一个基于HCI和AV-人类交互文献的初步关系原型分类法，并构建了一个可用于探索人机交互问题的理论框架。

Conclusion: 本研究旨在加强AVs与AI Agents这两个研究社区之间的桥梁（两者均具有自主性、快速采用、高影响力和经济转型潜力等共同特征）。期望激发学界对AI Agents社会影响不同类型的讨论，并邀请其他研究者扩展AI Agents比较分析的适用范围。

Abstract: Over the last couple of years, AI Agents have gained significant traction due to substantial progress in the capabilities of underlying General Purpose AI (GPAI) models, enhanced scaffolding techniques, and the promise to drive societal transformation. Companies, researchers, and policy makers have started to consider the different effects that AI agents may have across different dimensions of our lives. However, the literature exploring the broader effects of human-agent interactions is still underdeveloped. In this paper, we review the problem of traffic modulation by autonomous vehicles (AVs) in mixed traffic flows and extrapolate the learnings to the different modes of interaction between humans and AVs to the pair humans-AI agents. In doing so, we propose a preliminary taxonomy of relational archetypes based on literature on Human-Computer Interaction (HCI) and AV-human interaction and tentatively explore how the resulting framework may lead to new questions regarding human-agent interactions. Our effort is aimed at strengthening existing bridges between these two research communities, which share similar traits: autonomy, fast adoption, high impact, and great potential for economic transformation. Building on previous analogies between AI Agents and AVs (e.g., regarding autonomy levels), we anticipate this paper to spark scholarly debate on the different types of impact that agents may have on our societies, while inviting other researchers to expand the scope of their comparative analysis regarding AI Agents.

</details>


### [132] [RFID-Based Non-Biometric Classroom Attendance System: Proxy Attendance Detection via Weight Sensor Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22697)
*Furkan Ege,Muhsin Özdemir*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种无需生物特征识别的物联网考勤系统，通过RFID卡结合重量传感器验证来防止代打卡问题。该系统成本低廉、可复制性强，符合欧洲GDPR、土耳其KVKK和美国FERPA等数据保护法规的要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统考勤方式存在结构性缺陷，消耗教学时间并威胁学术诚信。小学和初高中考勤耗时数分钟，高等教育超过十分钟。此外，代打卡问题也是严重隐患。大多数现有电子考勤系统依赖生物特征认证，在GDPR、KVKK和FERPA等法规下存在法律和伦理风险，而单纯RFID系统无法防止代打卡。

Method: 系统由RFID模块、RFID卡、重量传感器、蓝牙模块和Arduino UNO微控制器组成。学生刷RFID卡后，重量传感器测量数据与来自三个Kaggle数据集的350名个体（18-22岁）的统计参考范围进行比对（不记录个人生物特征数据）。通过Python GUI进行学生管理、课程跟踪和基于蓝牙的CSV报表生成。

Result: 在接近真实教室环境的条件下进行定性测试，结果表明RFID读取、重量验证、蓝牙通信和GUI模块能够按预期集成运行。

Conclusion: 该系统提出了一种低成本的生物特征-free解决方案，可减少代打卡现象而不存储生物特征数据，具有可复制性，适合教育机构部署。

Abstract: Attendance tracking in educational institutions, when conducted through traditional methods, leads to structural problems that consume instruction time and threaten academic integrity. Attendance durations spanning several minutes in primary and secondary education and exceeding ten minutes in higher education, combined with the proxy attendance problem of signing on behalf of someone else, demonstrate the need for electronic systems. Most existing electronic solutions rely on biometric authentication, which raises legal and ethical risks under the European General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR), the Turkish Personal Data Protection Law (KVKK), and the United States Family Educational Rights and Privacy Act (FERPA). Systems using RFID alone provide no built-in safeguard against proxy attendance through card transfer.
  This study proposes a biometric-free IoT attendance system addressing both deficiencies. The prototype consists of an RFID module, RFID cards, weight sensors, a Bluetooth module, and an Arduino UNO microcontroller. After the student presents their RFID card, the weight sensor measurement is compared against a statistical reference range of 350 individuals (aged 18-22) compiled from three Kaggle datasets; no personal biometric data is recorded. A Python-based GUI performs student management, course tracking, and CSV-based reporting via Bluetooth.
  Qualitative tests in conditions close to a real classroom have shown that the RFID reading, weight verification, Bluetooth communication, and GUI modules operate in an integrated manner as expected. The proposed system offers a low-cost and reproducible solution that aims to reduce proxy attendance without storing biometric data.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [DM$^3$-Nav: Decentralized Multi-Agent Multimodal Multi-Object Semantic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22014)
*Amin Kashiri,Atharva Jamsandekar,Yasin Yazıcıoğlu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: DM³-Nav是一个完全去中心化的多智能体语义导航系统，支持多模态开放词汇目标规范和多目标任务，通过临时点对点通信协调，无需中央协调器或全局地图，在HM3DSem场景和真实办公环境中验证了性能可匹配甚至超越集中式基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体导航系统通常依赖中央协调器、全球地图聚合或共享全局状态，存在单点故障风险，且难以扩展到动态和真实环境。开放词汇目标规范和多目标任务导航在实际应用中需求日益增长，但现有方法大多假设集中式架构，限制了其鲁棒性和灵活性。

Method: 提出完全去中心化的多智能体语义导航框架DM³-Nav，采用无同步的临时成对通信机制，交换局部地图、目标状态和导航意图；设计意图广播与距离加权前沿选择相结合的隐式任务分配机制以减少冗余探索；支持多模态开放词汇目标规范和多目标任务。

Result: 在HM3DSem场景（HM3Dv0.2和GOAT-Bench数据集）上的评估表明，DM³-Nav的性能可匹配或超越集中式和共享地图基线方法；在真实办公环境中使用两台移动机器人进行验证，证明了完全依赖机载传感和计算的成功部署能力。

Conclusion: DM³-Nav成功实现了无需中央协调的分布式多智能体导航，在保持去中心化优势（消除单点故障）的同时达到与集中式方法相当的性能，为真实世界的多智能体机器人系统提供了一种鲁棒、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: We present DM$^3$-Nav, a fully decentralized multi-agent semantic navigation system supporting multimodal open-vocabulary goal specification and multi-object missions. In our setting, decentralization implies operation without a central coordinator, global map aggregation, or shared global state at runtime. Robots operate autonomously and coordinate through ad-hoc pairwise communication, exchanging local maps, goal status, and navigation intent without synchronization. An implicit task allocation mechanism combining intent broadcasting and distance-weighted frontier selection reduces redundant exploration while preserving decentralized operation. Evaluations on HM3DSem scenes using the HM3Dv0.2 and GOAT-Bench datasets demonstrate that DM$^3$-Nav matches or exceeds centralized and shared-map baselines while eliminating single points of failure inherent in centralized architectures. Finally, we validate our approach in a real-world office environment using two mobile robots, demonstrating successful deployment relying entirely on onboard sensing and computation. A video of our real-world experiments is available online: https://drive.google.com/file/d/1QiUSCn5rIvtuTUqtuXLPgmt6S8x9-MCZ/view?usp=drive_link

</details>


### [134] [Seeing the Whole Elephant: A Benchmark for Failure Attribution in LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22708)
*Mengzhuo Chen,Junjie Wang,Fangwen Mu,Yawen Wang,Zhe Liu,Huanxiang Feng,Qing Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了TraceElephant，一个基于完整执行轨迹的LLM多智能体系统故障归因基准。研究表明，相比仅使用部分观测（智能体输出），完整轨迹（包括输入和上下文）能将归因准确率提升高达76%，证实了缺失输入会掩盖许多故障根因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有故障归因基准仅依赖部分可观测轨迹（仅捕获智能体输出），缺少开发者调试时实际使用的输入和上下文信息。作者认为故障归因应在完整执行可观测性条件下研究，以贴合真实开发者调试场景。

Method: 构建TraceElephant基准，包含完整执行轨迹和可复现环境；对多种配置的故障归因技术进行系统评估；对比完整轨迹与部分观测条件下的归因效果。

Result: 完整轨迹相比部分观测可将归因准确率提升高达76%，证实缺失输入信息会掩盖大量故障原因。

Conclusion: TraceElephant为后续故障归因研究奠定基础，推动符合真实调试场景的评估实践，并支持更透明的多智能体系统开发。

Abstract: Failure attribution, i.e., identifying the responsible agent and decisive step of a failure, is particularly challenging in LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) due to their natural-language reasoning, nondeterministic outputs, and intricate interaction dynamics. A reliable benchmark is therefore essential to guide and evaluate attribution techniques. Yet existing benchmarks rely on partially observable traces that capture only agent outputs, omitting the inputs and context that developers actually use when debugging. We argue that failure attribution should be studied under full execution observability, aligning with real-world developer-facing scenarios where complete traces, rather than only outputs, are accessible for diagnosis. To this end, we introduce TraceElephant, a benchmark designed for failure attribution with full execution traces and reproducible environments. We then systematically evaluate failure attribution techniques across various configurations. Specifically, full traces improve attribution accuracy by up to 76\% over a partial-observation counterpart, confirming that missing inputs obscure many failure causes. TraceElephant provides a foundation for follow-up failure attribution research, promoting evaluation practices that reflect real-world debugging and supporting the development of more transparent MASs.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Feedback Over Form: Why Execution Feedback Matters More Than Pipeline Topology in 1-3B Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21950)
*Charles Junichi McAndrews*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究1-3B参数的小型语言模型能否通过流水线组合（尤其是加入执行反馈的自我改进机制）来弥补单模型在代码生成任务上的能力不足。通过NEAT启发的进化搜索，在HumanEval和MBPP基准上发现：执行反馈是关键增益来源（提升超过4个标准差），但复杂流水线拓扑结构并未带来显著收益；代码专用模型优于通用模型；提前停止至关重要；单次评估的适应度会高估结果5-7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型（1-3B）虽便于本地运行，但在复杂代码生成任务上能力有限。研究动机在于探索通过模型组合能否恢复部分损失的能力，以及更复杂的流水线结构是否优于简单的生成-执行-改进循环。

Method: 使用NEAT启发的进化搜索构建代码生成流水线，模型池包含1-3B参数的一般用途和代码专用模型。实验在HumanEval（164题）和MBPP（427题）上进行，均为本地单笔记本推理。核心流水线为"生成-执行-改进"循环，辅以执行反馈。

Result: 自我改进（带执行反馈）使代码生成提升超过4个标准差。收益主要来自修复运行时错误（NameError、SyntaxError），而非逻辑错误（AssertionError）。1.5B生成器+3B改进器的组合与3B模型兼任两角色效果相当。代码专用模型优于所有通用模型配置。进化搜索在受限搜索空间中主要重新发现了简单的手动设计循环，未从额外拓扑中获益。单次评估适应度导致结果高估5-7%。

Conclusion: 在1-3B规模下，执行反馈对流水线组合的增益作用大于复杂流水线拓扑。模型专业化比流水线架构更重要。早停机制不可或缺。进化搜索在此约束空间内未能发现超越简单"生成-执行-改进"循环的更优结构。

Abstract: Small language models (1-3B) are practical to run locally, but individually limited on harder code generation tasks. We ask whether composing them into pipelines can recover some of that lost capability. We study code generation pipelines built from 1-3B models with execution feedback, and use a NEAT-inspired evolutionary search to test whether more complex pipeline structure helps beyond a simple refinement loop. We evaluate on HumanEval (164 problems) and sanitized MBPP (427 problems), all with local inference on a single laptop. Self-refinement with execution feedback improves code generation by more than 4 standard deviations on both benchmarks. The gains are narrow in mechanism: refinement fixes many runtime errors (especially NameError and SyntaxError), but rarely fixes logic errors such as AssertionError. Within our tested general-purpose model pool, generator identity mattered less than refiner capability: a 1.5B generator paired with a 3B refiner matched a 3B model doing both roles. Early stopping is essential; without it, every iteration is net-negative. The code-specialized models outperform every general-purpose pipeline configuration, suggesting model specialization matters more than pipeline architecture. Preliminary text-only pipeline experiments without execution feedback did not show gains at this scale. In our constrained search space, evolutionary search mostly rediscovered the same simple generate-execute-refine loop we found manually, with no clearly significant gain from added topology. Single-evaluation fitness inflates results by 5-7 percent, selecting lucky genomes over good ones. On these benchmarks at 1-3B scale, execution feedback mattered more than added pipeline complexity in determining whether composition helped.

</details>


### [136] [Documentless Assessments Using Nominal Group Interviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22003)
*Eduardo Miranda*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需文档支撑的团体访谈技术，通过借鉴敏捷开发中的用户故事和计划扑克方法，有效促进了过程评估参与者的协作与共识达成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的过程评估方法过度依赖文档审查和审计式访谈，缺乏促进参与者协作的机制，难以有效收集和验证信息，尤其在参与者存在分歧的情况下更难达成共识。

Method: 该方法创新性地将敏捷开发中的"用户故事"概念用于将CMMI特定实践转化为具体表述，并采用"计划扑克"（Planning Poker）技术替代传统的文档审查和审计访谈来进行事实发现与验证。

Result: 该技术已在一次咨询项目中成功应用，有效促使原本存在分歧的参与者相互交流并就关键问题达成一致。

Conclusion: 该技术能够有效地将原本分散甚至对立的参与者引导至协作讨论并达成共识，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper describes a group interview technique designed to support documentless process assessments while promoting at the same time collaboration among assessment participants. The method was successfully used in one consulting assignment where it got previously discording participants, talking to each other and agreeing on the issues. The technique borrows from agile software development the concept of user stories to cast CMMIs specific practices in concrete terms and the Planning Poker technique, instead of document reviews and audit like interviews, for fact finding and corroboration.

</details>


### [137] [FlyCatcher: Neural Inference of Runtime Checkers from Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22028)
*Beatriz Souza,Chang Lou,Suman Nath,Michael Pradel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出FlyCatcher，一种从现有测试用例自动推导运行时检查器的自动化方法，通过结合LLM合成、静态分析和动态验证，从测试中提取状态ful检查器，用于检测复杂软件系统中的静默故障。实验表明该方法比现有方法多推断2.6倍正确检查器，能检测5.2倍更多错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂软件系统常遭受静默故障（silent failures）困扰，即不引发显式错误的语义违规。现有运行时检查器方法需要人工编写，开发成本高，实际应用有限。现有测试用例是宝贵的语义信息来源，但如何从中自动提取可推广到任意执行的检查器仍是关键挑战。

Method: FlyCatcher采用三阶段方法：(1) LLM-based synthesis: 利用大语言模型从测试用例中推断检查器；(2) 静态分析: 对生成的检查器进行代码级分析验证；(3) 动态验证: 在实际运行中验证检查器的正确性。检查器具有状态维护能力，通过维护shadow state来抽象实际系统状态。

Result: 在4个复杂软件系统的400个测试用例上评估，FlyCatcher成功推断出334个检查器，其中300个通过交叉验证确认为正确。与state-of-the-art方法相比，FlyCatcher多推断出2.6倍正确检查器，并能检测5.2倍更多错误。

Conclusion: 本文实现了从测试用例自动推断运行时检查器的完整方法，为复杂软件系统中静默故障的检测提供了实用化途径，有望推动运行时检查技术的广泛应用。

Abstract: Complex software systems often suffer from silent failures, i.e., violations of the intended semantics that do not cause explicit errors. A promising approach to detect such errors is to use system-specific runtime checkers that monitor the execution of a system and check for violations of the intended semantics. However, writing such checkers for a given software system is challenging and time-consuming, and hence, rarely done in practice. This work presents FlyCatcher, an automated approach to derive runtime checkers from existing tests, i.e., from a resource available for most software systems. The critical challenge of such an approach is to generalize the behavioral properties encoded in a test case to arbitrary executions of a system. FlyCatcher addresses this challenge through a combination of LLM-based synthesis, static analysis, and dynamic validation, which infers a checker that monitors specific method calls and asserts properties that should hold when they are called. The inferred checkers are stateful, i.e., they reason about the system's behavior by maintaining a shadow state that abstracts the actual system state as needed by the checker. Our evaluation applies FlyCatcher to 400 tests from four widely used, complex software systems. The approach infers 334 checkers, out of which 300 are found to be correct via cross-validation. Compared with a state-of-the-art approach, our approach infers 2.6x more correct checkers, which enables it to detect 5.2x more errors. By contributing to the automated inference of runtime checkers from tests, this work enables the broader adoption of runtime checking as a practical approach to detect silent failures in complex software systems.

</details>


### [138] [Call-Chain-Aware LLM-Based Test Generation for Java Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22046)
*Guancheng Wang,Qinghua Xu,Lionel C. Briand,Zhaoqiang Guo,Kui Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CAT是一种基于调用链感知的LLM测试生成方法，通过静态分析将调用链和依赖上下文显式融入提示词，以解决复杂软件系统中深度调用链和对象初始化带来的挑战。该方法在Defects4J基准和4个真实GitHub项目上进行了评估，相比SOTA方法PANTA，代码覆盖率分别提升18.04%和21.74%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的LLM测试生成方法主要依赖执行路径信息来引导提示词构建，但对于具有丰富类间依赖、深度调用链和复杂对象初始化要求的复杂软件系统，这种方法往往不够充分。

Method: CAT通过专门的静态分析将调用链和依赖上下文显式融入提示词。具体包括：1) 系统性建模调用者-被调用者关系、对象构造函数和第三方依赖；2) 支持迭代式测试修复以处理生成失败。

Result: 在Defects4J基准和4个LLM截断日期后发布的真实GitHub项目上，CAT相比PANTA在行覆盖率和分支覆盖率上分别提升18.04%和21.74%，且在真实项目中持续表现优异。消融实验验证了调用链和依赖上下文的重要性。

Conclusion: 调用链和依赖上下文的显式建模对于LLM生成高质量测试用例至关重要，CAT为复杂软件系统的自动化测试生成提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently shown strong potential for generating project-level unit tests. However, existing state-of-the-art approaches primarily rely on execution-path information to guide prompt construction, which is often insufficient for complex software systems with rich inter-class dependencies, deep call chains, and intricate object initialization requirements. In this paper, we present CAT, a novel call-chain-aware LLM-based test generation approach that explicitly incorporates call-chain and dependency contexts into prompts through dedicated static analysis. To construct executable, semantically valid test contexts, CAT systematically models caller--callee relationships, object constructors, and third-party dependencies, and supports iterative test fixing when generation failures occur. We evaluate CAT on the widely used Defects4J benchmark and on four real-world GitHub projects released after the LLM's cut-off date. The results show that, across projects in Defects4J, CAT improves line and branch coverage by 18.04% and 21.74%, respectively, over the state-of-the-art approach PANTA, while consistently achieving superior performance on post-cutoff real-world projects. An ablation study further demonstrates the importance of call-chain and dependency contexts in CAT.

</details>


### [139] [TRACE: Topology-aware Reconstruction of Accidents in CARLA for AV Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22068)
*Nahian Salsabil,Sebastian Elbaum*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE方法，通过自动化重建NHTSA事故报告为高保真CARLA仿真来解决自动驾驶车辆验证中罕见安全关键场景缺失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶车辆验证面临的核心挑战是：真实驾驶数据中罕见安全关键场景获取困难。现有基准测试仅生成合成冲突场景或基于抽象道路几何映射事故描述，无法捕获真实世界事故的拓扑复杂性。

Method: TRACE pipeline包含三个核心步骤：(1) 检索站点特异性OpenStreetMap数据以保留精确道路拓扑；(2) 利用大语言模型从道路几何和碰撞前机动动作推断车辆初始状态；(3) 从半结构化报告数据生成仿真轨迹。最终构建了包含52个多样化事故场景的基准测试。

Result: 创建了涵盖多种碰撞类型、道路拓扑和碰撞前机动动作的52个多样化事故场景基准测试，提供了一个具有挑战性的开源资源用于测试自动驾驶系统。

Conclusion: TRACE通过将NHTSA事故报告自动化重建为高保真仿真，有效解决了现有方法在捕获真实世界事故拓扑复杂性方面的不足，为自动驾驶系统测试提供了更贴近现实的验证资源。

Abstract: Validating Autonomous Vehicles (AVs) requires exposure to rare, safety-critical scenarios, infrequent in routine driving data. Existing benchmarks address this by generating synthetic conflicts or mapping accident descriptions to abstract road geometries, failing to capture the topological complexity of real-world crashes. We introduce TRACE , a pipeline that automates the reconstruction of NHTSA crash reports into high-fidelity CARLA simulations by (1) retrieving site-specific OpenStreetMap data to preserve exact road topology, (2) leveraging Large Language Models to infer vehicles' initial state from road geometry and pre-crash maneuvers, and (3) generating simulation trajectories from semi-structured report data. Using this pipeline, we curated a benchmark of 52 diverse accident scenarios covering varied collision types, road topologies, and pre-crash maneuvers, providing a challenging open source resource for testing AV systems against real-world failures.

</details>


### [140] [Ethics Testing: Proactive Identification of Generative AI System Harms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22089)
*Shin Hwei Tan,Haibo Wang,Heng Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GAI) systems that can automatically generate content in the form of source code or other contents (e.g., images) has seen increasing popularity due to the emergence of tools such as ChatGPT which rely on Large Language Models (LLMs). Misuse of the automatically generated content can incur serious consequences due to potential harms in the generated content. Despite the importance of ensuring the quality of automatically generated content, there is little to no approach that can systematically generate tests for identifying software harms in the content generated by these GAI systems. In this article, we introduce the novel concept of ethics testing which aims to systematically generate tests for identifying software harms. Different from existing testing methodologies (e.g., fairness testing that aims to identifying software discrimination), ethics testing aims to systematically detect software harms that could be induced due to unethical behavior (e.g., harmful behavior or behavior that violates intellectual property rights) in automatically generated content. We introduced the concept of ethics testing, discussed the challenges therewithin, and conducted five case studies to show how ethics testing can be performed for generative AI systems.

</details>


### [141] [Evaluating LLM-Based Goal Extraction in Requirements Engineering: Prompting Strategies and Their Limitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22207)
*Anna Arnaudo,Riccardo Coppola,Maurizio Morisio,Flavio Giobergia,Andrea Bioddo,Angelo Bongiorno,Luca Dadone*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出使用LLM链式提示从软件文档中提取功能目标的自动化方法，实现GORE流程的三阶段处理（角色识别、高低层目标提取），并在低层目标识别达到61%准确率，表明该方法更适合作为人工提取的辅助工具而非完全替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于需求工程（RE）制品具有文本化和重复性特征，LLM可有效自动化其生成与处理；GORE流程中的功能目标提取为耗时的手动任务，存在自动化需求。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线（角色识别、高层/低层目标提取），使用链式LLM与工程化提示；测试不同上下文学习变体，测量输入数据与示例的相似度；实现生成-批评（generation-critic）反馈循环机制。

Result: 低层目标识别准确率达61%；反馈循环结合Zero-shot优于独立Few-shot；消融实验表明无反馈循环性能略有下降；反馈机制与Few-shot结合未带来优势。

Conclusion: 该方法最适合作为加速手动提取的工具，而非完全替代方案。反馈机制与Few-shot结合无优势，提示策略可能存在性能上限。未来将整合RAG和CoT提示以提升准确率。

Abstract: Due to the textual and repetitive nature of many Requirements Engineering (RE) artefacts, Large Language Models (LLMs) have proven useful to automate their generation and processing. In this paper, we discuss a possible approach for automating the Goal-Oriented Requirements Engineering (GORE) process by extracting functional goals from software documentation through three phases: actor identification, high and low-level goal extraction. To implement these functionalities, we propose a chain of LLMs fed with engineered prompts. We experimented with different variants of in-context learning and measured the similarities between input data and in-context examples to better investigate their impact. Another key element is the generation-critic mechanism, implemented as a feedback loop involving two LLMs. Although the pipeline achieved 61% accuracy in low-level goal identification, the final stage, these results indicate the approach is best suited as a tool to accelerate manual extraction rather than as a full replacement. The feedback-loop mechanism with Zero-shot outperformed stand-alone Few-shot, with an ablation study suggesting that performance slightly degrades without the feedback cycle. However, we reported that the combination of the feedback mechanism with Few-shot does not deliver any advantage, possibly suggesting that the primary performance ceiling is the prompting strategy applied to the 'critic' LLM. Together with the refinement of both the quantity and quality of the Shot examples, future research will integrate Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting to improve accuracy.

</details>


### [142] [RAG-Reflect: Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Reflections for Comment-Driven Code Maintenance on Stack Overflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22217)
*Mehedi Hasan Shanto,Muhammad Asaduzzaman,Alioune Ngom*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RAG-Reflect是一个无需任务特定训练的模块化框架，通过结合检索增强推理与自反思机制，在有效评论-编辑预测任务上实现接近微调模型的性能(F1=0.78)，证明了agentic AI原则在软件维护任务中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stack Overflow等平台上用户评论大多为表达感谢或寻求澄清，仅有小部分能驱动有意义的代码编辑。有效识别哪些评论真正触发了代码编辑对于软件维护至关重要，但现有方法难以在不进行任务特定训练的情况下实现高精度预测。

Method: RAG-Reflect框架采用三阶段运行时工作流：(1) 检索相关上下文示例；(2) 推理评论与代码编辑之间的因果关系；(3) 基于初始化阶段生成的验证规则进行决策反思。其中初始化阶段由Interpretation模块分析知识库生成验证规则。

Result: 在SOUP基准测试集上达到：Precision=0.81, Recall=0.74, F1=0.78，优于传统基线方法（Logistic Regression、XGBoost、各种提示技术），且接近微调模型的性能(F1=0.773)。消融实验表明检索模块和反思模块均显著提升性能。

Conclusion: RAG-Reflect demonstrates that agentic AI principles (retrieval + reasoning + reflection) can effectively handle software maintenance tasks without task-specific training, achieving near fine-tuned model performance while maintaining flexibility and modularity.

Abstract: User comments on online programming platforms such as Stack Overflow play a vital role in maintaining the correctness and relevance of shared code examples. However, the majority of comments express gratitude or clarification, while only a small fraction highlight actionable issues that drive meaningful edits. This paper demonstrates how agentic AI principles can revolutionize software maintenance tasks by presenting RAG-Reflect, a modular framework that achieves fine-tuned-level performance for valid comment-edit prediction without task-specific training. Valid Comment-Edit Prediction (VCP) is the task of determining whether a user comment directly triggered a subsequent code edit. The framework integrates large language models (LLMs) with retrieval-augmented reasoning and self-reflection mechanisms. RAG-Reflect operates through a three-stage runtime workflow built on a one-time pattern analysis phase. During initialization, an Interpretation module analyzes the knowledge base to generate validation rules. At inference time, the system (1) retrieves contextual examples, (2) reasons about comment-edit causality, and (3) reflects on decisions using the pre-established rules. We evaluate RAG-Reflect on the publicly available SOUP benchmark, achieving Precision = 0.81, Recall = 0.74, and F1 = 0.78, outperforming traditional baselines (e.g., Logistic Regression, XGBoost, different prompting techniques) and closely approaching the performance of fine-tuned models (F1 = 0.773) without retraining. Our ablation and stage-level analyses show that both retrieval and reflection modules substantially enhance performance.

</details>


### [143] [Enhancing a gamified tool for UML modeling education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22400)
*Giacomo Garaccione,Riccardo Coppola,Luca Ardito*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文描述了对UMLegend这一UML建模教育游戏化工具的改进，扩展其支持多种建模语言（包括用例图），并重构为模块化架构，为2026年的纵向研究奠定基础，以评估游戏化学习对学生的长期影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生在软件工程教育中低估了UML建模的重要性，现有游戏化工具几乎仅关注类图，忽视了用例图和其他建模表示法的支持需求，导致建模教育缺乏吸引力。

Method: 基于2025年实验结果，对UMLegend进行增强设计：采用模块化架构重构，便于扩展其他软件工程主题和建模表示法；新增用例图练习类型；规划2026年纵向研究以评估长期效果。

Result: 成功将UMLegend从单一类图支持扩展为支持多种建模语言，模块化架构使添加新建模表示法更加便捷，为广泛的教育应用奠定技术基础。

Conclusion: 增强后的UMLegend有望通过游戏化机制提升学生对UML建模的学习兴趣和实践能力；2026年的纵向研究将提供长期有效性证据，验证游戏化教育能否持续改善学生表现。

Abstract: Unified Modeling Language (UML) Use Case and Class Diagrams are fundamental modeling notations in Software Engineering (SE) education due to their importance for requirements and model-based engineering, yet their relevance is underestimated by students, who tend to dismiss the topic as secondary. Gamification has been adopted to make modeling education more appealing, but existing tools focus almost exclusively on class diagrams, leaving support for use cases and other notations unexplored. In 2025, we designed UMLegend, a gamified tool for class diagrams that offered dynamic feedback to help students learn correct modeling practices and multiple long-term mechanics to increase engagement, and performed a study with the tool. With this paper, we describe how we enhanced UMLegend following the results of the experiment so that it can support more modeling languages, with use case diagrams being added to the type of available exercises in the tool. The revised version has been refactored to have a modular architecture, to make it easier to add other software engineering topics and additional modeling notations. We also describe the potential impact we expect the new version to have, and outline a longitudinal study we intend to perform in 2026 where we will assess whether long-term UML gamification leads to improved student performance.

</details>


### [144] [Gamifying Architectural Governance to Reduce Organizational Coupling in Microservice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22454)
*Xiaozhou Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究提出通过游戏化机制治理微服务系统中的组织耦合（OC），构建一个持续挖掘代码仓库数据、检测架构边界违规行为的游戏化框架，结合积分、徽章、排行榜和架构改进任务等游戏元素，引导开发者维护服务边界，提升架构可维护性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务架构虽支持模块化和可扩展性，但实践中开发者跨服务贡献频繁，导致组织耦合侵蚀架构边界、增加协调开销。传统技术手段难以有效约束开发者行为，需要引入行为激励机制来解决这一 socio-technical 问题。

Method: 框架核心包括三层：①数据挖掘层——持续从代码仓库提取贡献者-服务关联数据；②架构度量层——检测架构边界违反和依赖关系增长；③游戏化层——将检测结果转化为游戏元素（积分、徽章、排行榜）和架构改进任务。整体形成"检测→激励→引导"的闭环。

Result: 论文为概念性工作，尚未开展实证评估。作者提出了评估路线图，计划从两个维度衡量效果：①游戏化组织耦合治理的技术效能（如OC指数变化）；②开发者参与度和行为改变程度。

Conclusion: 本研究开创了软件架构治理、社会技术分析与游戏化交叉的新研究方向，论证了行为激励对支持可持续微服务演化的潜力。贡献包括概念框架、检测机制设计和评估路线图。

Abstract: Microservice is a popular software architecture that relies on decentralized teams and clear service ownership to support modularity and scalability. However, in practice, developers frequently contribute across multiple services, creating organizational coupling (OC) that gradually erodes architectural boundaries and increases coordination overhead. This study proposes a vision for behavior-driven architectural governance through gamification in microservice systems to influence developer behavior and reduce OC. Our approach introduces a gamified framework that continuously mines repository data to detect architectural boundary violations and increasing service dependencies, and translates those signals into gameful designs, including points, badges, leaderboards, and architecture improvement quests. We outline a conceptual framework that integrates repository mining, architectural metrics, and gamification mechanisms to encourage developers to maintain service boundaries and improve architectural maintainability. Furthermore, we present an evaluation roadmap to assess the impact of gamified OC governance and developer engagement. This work aims to open a new research direction at the intersection of software architecture governance, socio-technical analysis, and gamification, highlighting the potential of behavioral incentives to support sustainable microservice evolution.

</details>


### [145] [Test Design and Review Argumentation in AI-Assisted Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22473)
*Eduard Paul Enoiu,Robert Feldt*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种概念分类法和结构化模板，用于AI辅助测试用例生成，通过从测试目标、主张、理由和证据四个维度表征测试用例，旨在帮助工程师理解决定生成测试用例的论证过程，而非评估测试用例本身的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI助手能越来越高效地生成和演进测试用例，主要挑战已从生成能力转向理解生成原因。现有工作主要集中在测试技术分类、需求-测试关联和系统保证论证结构化等方面，但缺乏对个体测试设计决策背后论证过程的显式表示，导致工程师难以理解AI生成的测试用例为何存在及其依据。

Method: 提出了一个概念分类法（conceptual taxonomy）和结构化模板（structured template），从四个维度表征测试用例：测试目标（test goal）、主张（claim）、理由（reason）和证据（evidence）。该方法适用于测试设计阶段的建构性使用和审查阶段的回顾性使用。

Result: 该分类法可同时服务于两个用途：在测试设计阶段作为指导工具，在审查阶段用于评估附加论证的质量，而非评估生成测试用例的合理性或客观价值。

Conclusion: 该研究为AI辅助测试生成提供了论证层面的透明度，有助于工程师理解、评估和改进AI生成的测试设计决策。

Abstract: AI assistants can increasingly generate and evolve test cases. The challenge is no longer merely to produce them, but also to help engineers understand why a generated artefact exists and what supports it. Existing work has focused on classifying testing techniques, linking requirements to tests and structuring system assurance arguments, but it does not explicitly represent the argumentation behind individual test design decisions. We propose a conceptual taxonomy and a structured template for AI-assisted test generation that characterizes a test case by its test goal, claim, reason, and evidence. The taxonomy is intended for both constructive use during test design and retrospective use during review, to assess the quality of the attached argument rather than the plausibility or objective value of the generated test cases.

</details>


### [146] [A Comparison of ROS 2 and AUTOSAR Adaptive Platform Against Industry-Elicited Automotive Middleware Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22576)
*Lucas Hegerath,David Philipp Klüner,Philipp Pelcz,Viswanatha Reddy Batchu,Marius Molz,Julius Kahle,Thomas Schulik,Stefan Kowalewski,Alexandru Kampmann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对比评估了ROS 2 Jazzy与AUTOSAR Adaptive Platform R24-11两款主流汽车中间件框架，基于ZF Group汽车工程师的实际需求，分析其对软件定义汽车实际应用的适用性，旨在为中间件的开发与评估提供工业界视角的参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件定义汽车中，中间件在实现软件组件间的高效通信、集成和协调方面起着基础性作用。工业界迫切需要了解现有主流中间件框架对实际需求的满足程度。

Method: 从ZF Group汽车供应商的软件工程师那里收集实际需求，然后对两个中间件框架进行评估对比。

Result: 研究揭示了这两个主流框架在满足实际工业需求方面的表现差异。

Conclusion: 研究为汽车应用中间件的开发与评估提供了工业视角的参考依据。

Abstract: In software-defined vehicles, automotive middleware plays a fundamental role in enabling efficient communication, integration, and coordination among software components. This paper examines how well two of the currently most popular middleware frameworks, ROS 2 Jazzy and AUTOSAR Adaptive Platform R24-11, meet practical requirements elicited from automotive software engineers at one of the major automotive supplier companies, ZF Group. Our objective is to provide insight into an otherwise difficult-to-obtain industrial perspective and support a clearer understanding of priorities in the development and evaluation of middleware for automotive applications.

</details>


### [147] [From Natural Language to Verified Code: Toward AI Assisted Problem-to-Code Generation with Dafny-Based Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22601)
*Md Erfan,Md Kamal Hossain Chowdhury,Ahmed Ryan,Md Rayhanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了NL2VC-60数据集（60个复杂算法问题），用于评估LLM在形式化验证中的表现。通过层级提示策略（无上下文、签名引导、自修复），Gemma 4-31B达到90.91%验证成功率，GPT-OSS 120B从0%提升至81.82%。研究表明形式化验证现已可用于开源LLM辅助高保证软件开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在自动化软件工程中前景广阔，但常产生错误或幻觉代码。现有形式化验证方法需要将自然语言转换为精确的形式化规约，这一过渡过程仍十分困难。

Method: 1) 构建NL2VC-60数据集，包含60个复杂算法问题；2) 使用层级提示策略评估7个开源LLM：上下文无关提示、结构签名提示、自修复提示（结合Dafny验证器的迭代反馈）；3) 集成uDebug平台防止"空洞验证"（用平凡规约满足验证器）。

Result: 上下文无关提示几乎全部失败；结构签名和迭代自修复带来显著性能提升。Gemma 4-31B达到90.91%验证成功率；GPT-OSS 120B在签名引导反馈下从0%提升至81.82%。

Conclusion: 形式化验证对开源LLM已切实可行，开源LLM可作为有效"学徒"用于合成复杂规约并推动高保证软件开发。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise in automated software engineering, yet their guarantee of correctness is frequently undermined by erroneous or hallucinated code. To enforce model honesty, formal verification requires LLMs to synthesize implementation logic alongside formal specifications that are subsequently proven correct by a mathematical verifier. However, the transition from informal natural language to precise formal specification remains an arduous task. Our work addresses this by providing the NaturalLanguage2VerifiedCode (NL2VC)-60 dataset: a collection of 60 complex algorithmic problems. We evaluate 11 randomly selected problem sets across seven open-weight LLMs using a tiered prompting strategy: contextless prompts, signature prompts providing structural anchors, and self-healing prompts utilizing iterative feedback from the Dafny verifier. To address vacuous verification, where models satisfy verifiers with trivial specifications, we integrate the uDebug platform to ensure functional validation. Our results show that while contextless prompting leads to near-universal failure, structural signatures and iterative self-healing facilitate a dramatic performance turnaround. Specifically, Gemma 4-31B achieved a 90.91\% verification success rate, while GPT-OSS 120B rose from zero to 81.82\% success with signature-guided feedback. These findings indicate that formal verification is now attainable for open-weight LLMs, which serve as effective apprentices for synthesizing complex annotations and facilitating high-assurance software development.

</details>


### [148] [Quality-Driven Selective Mutation for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22640)
*Zaheed Ahmed,Emmanuel Charleson Dapaah,Philip Makedonski,Jens Grabowski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种概率框架，从抵抗性（hard-to-kill）和真实性（Jaccard相似度）两个维度量化深度学习变异体质量，实现无特定用例假设的变异算子配置优选。实验验证该方法可显著降低测试成本而不损害变异体有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习选择性变异测试面临成本与质量权衡问题：需在减少变异体生成和执行成本的同时，保留抵抗性（指导测试改进）和真实性（模拟真实bug）两个核心能力。现有DL文献缺乏统一度量框架来同时捕捉这两个关键维度。

Method: 提出了基于概率框架的变异体质量量化方法，包含两个互补维度：1) 抵抗性（Resistance）——借鉴经典变异测试中"难杀死"概念，使用统计击杀概率；2) 真实性（Realism）——通过广义Jaccard相似度度量变异体与真实故障检测模式的相似程度。基于此框架对低质量算子配置进行排序和筛选，在4个真实DL缺陷数据集上验证效果。

Result: 实验结果表明：质量驱动选择策略可将生成的变异体数量减少最多55.6%，同时保持基线水平的抵抗性和真实性。

Conclusion: 研究成功验证了双目标选择框架的有效性：可在减少55.6%变异体的同时，保持足够的抵抗性和真实性，为深度学习变异测试提供了实用的成本-质量平衡方案。

Abstract: Mutants support testing and debugging in two roles: (i) as test goals and (ii) as substitutes for real faults. Hard-to-kill mutants provide better guidance for test improvement, while realism is essential when mutants are used to simulate real bugs. Building on these roles, selective mutation for deep learning (DL) aims to reduce the cost of mutant generation and execution by choosing operator configurations that yield resistant and realistic mutants. However, the DL literature lacks a unified measure that captures both aspects. This study presents a probabilistic framework to quantify mutant quality along two complementary axes: resistance and realism. Resistance adapts the classical notion of hard-to-kill mutants to the DL setting using statistical killing probabilities, while realism is measured via the generalized Jaccard similarity between mutant and real-fault detectability patterns. The framework enables ranking and filtering of low-quality mutation-operator configurations without assuming a specific use case. We empirically evaluate the approach on four datasets of real DL faults. Three datasets (CleanML, DeepFD, and DeepLocalize) are used to estimate and select high-quality operator configurations, and the held-out defect4ML dataset is used for validation. Results show that quality-driven selection reduces the number of generated mutants by up to 55.6% while preserving typical levels of resistance and realism under baseline-aligned selection thresholds. These findings confirm that dual-objective selection can lower cost without compromising the usefulness of mutants for either role.

</details>


### [149] [Verifier Warnings Do Not Improve Comprehensibility Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22653)
*Nadeeshan De Silva,Martin Kellogg,Oscar Chaparro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究验证了一个反直觉的假设：尽管代码复杂度与验证器警告存在相关性，但验证器警告总数并不能为代码可理解性预测提供额外的区分能力，传统语法特征和开发者特征已足够有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期元分析研究发现验证器警告总数与代码可理解性指标存在弱相关性，论文推测该特征可增强代码可理解性预测模型的性能。

Method: 控制-处理实验：向文献中的机器学习模型添加验证器警告总数特征，与仅使用语法特征和开发者特征的模型进行预测性能比较分析。

Result: 添加验证器警告总数特征的模型与未添加该特征的模型在预测性能上无显著差异。

Conclusion: 验证器警告总数对代码可理解性预测的区分能力有限，将语法特征和开发者特征组合已足以预测人工判断的代码可理解性。

Abstract: Proponents of software verification suggest that code simplicity is linked to the effort to verify code, hypothesizing that formal verifiers produce fewer false positive warnings and require less manual intervention when analyzing simpler code. A recent meta-analysis study found empirical support for this hypothesis: a small correlation between the sum of verifier warnings and human-derived code comprehensibility metrics. Based on this finding, we conjectured that using the sum of verifier tool (verifier) warnings to represent program semantic information as an input feature to machine learning (ML) models for code comprehensibility prediction can enhance their performance, when combined with traditional syntactic and developer features.
  To test this conjecture, we performed a control-treatment experiment incorporating the verifier warning sum feature into machine learning models from the literature, and conducted a comparative analysis of their performance against models trained only on syntactic and developer features. We found no significant difference in the prediction performance of models with and without the warnings feature. Our findings suggest that while a correlation exists, the verifier warning sum offers limited discriminative power: combining syntactic and developer features is just as effective for predicting human-judged code comprehensibility.

</details>


### [150] [RealBench: A Repo-Level Code Generation Benchmark Aligned with Real-World Software Development Practices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22659)
*Jia Li,Hongyi Deng,Yiran Zhang,Kechi Zhang,Tianqi Shao,Tiankuo Zhao,Weinan Wang,Zhi Jin,Ge Li,Yang Liu,Yingtao Fang,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RealBench是一个对齐真实行业实践的仓库级代码生成基准，每个示例同时包含自然语言需求和UML图。研究发现当前LLM在仓库级代码生成上表现不佳、模块生成质量差但善于定位模块、生成策略需因仓库规模而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准（如HumanEval、EvoCodeBench）仅基于自然语言描述评估LLM代码生成能力，但企业实际开发中，开发者更多基于结构化设计文档（如UML图）而非纯自然语言编写代码。当前基准与真实行业实践之间存在显著差距，无法准确反映代码生成对软件开发任务的实际帮助程度。

Method: 提出RealBench，一个与真实行业软件开发实践对齐的仓库级代码生成基准。该基准的每个示例同时包含自然语言需求和UML图作为系统设计，模拟开发者实际接收规格说明的方式。在此基准上对先进LLM进行系统性评估。

Result: 实验揭示三个关键发现：1）仓库级代码生成任务中LLM表现较差，且不同LLM间存在显著性能差距；2）LLM善于定位和创建UML图中定义的模块，但生成的模块质量较差，存在语法和逻辑错误；3）生成策略因仓库规模而异：小型仓库整体生成效果最佳，复杂仓库采用逐模块生成策略效果更好。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在基于结构化系统设计（UML图）的仓库级代码生成任务中存在明显局限。生成策略需根据仓库规模灵活选择：小型仓库适合整体生成，复杂仓库适合逐模块生成。RealBench为评估LLM在实际企业开发场景中的代码生成能力提供了更贴近现实的基准。

Abstract: Writing code requires significant time and effort in software development. To automate this process, researchers have made substantial progress using Large Language Models (LLMs) for code generation. Many benchmarks like HumanEval and EvoCodeBench have been created to evaluate LLMs by requiring them to generate code from natural language requirements. However, in enterprise applications and team development, developers typically write code based on structured designs or specifications rather than raw natural language descriptions. This gap between existing benchmarks and real industry development practices means that current benchmark scores may not accurately reflect how much code generation can help automate software development tasks.
  To address this gap, we propose RealBench, a repository-level code generation benchmark aligned with real-world industry software development practices. Each example includes both natural language requirements and UML diagrams as system design, matching how developers typically receive specifications. Based on the constructed benchmarks, we conduct a systematic evaluation of advanced LLMs' code generation capabilities when provided with structured system designs. The experimental results reveal key insights in current LLMs' capabilities for repo-level code generation aligned with real-world software development practices. First, we notice that regarding repo-level code generation, LLMs show much worse performance and there are significant performance gaps among LLMs. Second, LLMs are good at finding and creating modules defined in UML diagrams, but the quality of generated modules is often poor due to grammar and logic errors. Third, generating the entire repository at once is the best generation strategy on smaller repositories, while generating a complex repository with the module-by-module strategy works better compared to other strategies.

</details>


### [151] [Inferring Equivalence Classes from Legacy Undocumented Embedded Binaries for ISO 26262-Compliant Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22673)
*Marco De Luca,Domenico Francesco De Angelis,Domenico Amalfitano,Pasquale Cimmino,Anna Rita Fasolino*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种无需源码和文档即可从二进制固件推断输出导向等价类的方法，应用于工业汽车场景的遗留固件测试，验证了其可行性和实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISO 26262等安全标准要求使用等价类划分进行系统测试，但遗留嵌入式固件往往缺乏完整的功能规格文档，导致该技术难以应用。

Method: 结合控制流重构与引导式符号执行，分析二进制固件中的函数，基于可观测行为（返回值、输出参数）对执行路径进行等价类划分。

Result: 方法与专家预期高度一致，从业者对可读性和实用性评价积极，支持函数理解和测试设计。

Conclusion: 验证了二进制级等价类推断方法在工业汽车环境中的可行性和实用价值，为缺乏文档的遗留安全嵌入式软件的系统化测试提供了有效支撑。

Abstract: Equivalence class partitioning is a well-established test design technique mandated by safety standards such as ISO~26262 for systematic testing of safety software. In industrial practice, however, its application to legacy undocumented embedded firmware is often hindered by incomplete or outdated functional specifications.
  This paper proposes a binary-level methodology for inferring output-oriented equivalence classes directly from compiled firmware, without relying on source-level annotations or external documentation. The approach combines control-flow reconstruction and guided symbolic execution to analyze individual functions and group execution paths according to indistinguishable observable behavior, including return values and output parameters. An optional post-processing step produces human-readable representations to support comprehension and documentation.
  The methodology is evaluated in an industrial automotive context through a practitioner-based study assessing correctness and interpretability. Results indicate strong alignment with expert expectations and a positive perception of readability and usefulness for supporting function understanding and test design. These findings demonstrate the feasibility and practical relevance of binary-level equivalence class inference for systematic testing of legacy undocumented safety-embedded software.

</details>


### [152] [Code for All: Educational Applications of the "Vibe Coding" Hackathon in Programming Education across All Skill Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22747)
*Ashley J. Chen,Yijia Cao,Minghao Shao,Ramesh Karri,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一项关于"氛围编程"(vibe coding)的教育价值研究,通过一个月长的在线黑客松活动进行,参与者从零经验初学者到资深开发者不等。研究发现LLM辅助编程能够扩大编程学习的机会,同时揭示了不同背景参与者在任务复杂度增加时的参与模式差异,以及"禁止手动编辑"约束对提示工程和调试实践的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的出现使得"氛围编程"(vibe coding)成为可能,用户通过自然语言描述意图即可生成或修改代码。这一新范式可能扩大编程学习的可及性,但其教育效果尚不明确。研究旨在探究AI辅助编程在教育环境中的价值,以及如何将其有效整合到编程教育中。

Method: 采用混合研究设计,通过为期一个月的在线黑客松活动收集数据。参与者来自多个国家,涵盖从零经验初学者到资深开发者。黑客松设置三个难度递增的赛道(Spark/Build/Launch)。关键约束:仅使用LLM生成代码,禁止手动编辑。数据收集包括:完整聊天历史、源代码、演示视频、功能报告,以及项目评估(功能、UI/UX设计、影响力、提示质量、代码可读性)和后测问卷(含开放式反馈的主題分析)。

Result: 研究描述了:(1)不同背景参与者在任务复杂度增加时的参与模式;(2)"禁止手动编辑"约束如何塑造提示工程和调试实践;(3)这些模式对AI辅助开发整合到编程教育和竞赛学习环境的启示。

Conclusion: 氛围编程有潜力扩大编程学习的可及性,同时保持有意义的学习成果。研究为将AI辅助开发整合到编程教育中提供了实证依据,特别是在竞赛学习环境中如何平衡技术挑战与学习目标方面具有实践指导意义。

Abstract: The emergence of large language models has enabled vibe coding, a natural language approach to programming in which users describe intent and AI generates or revises code, potentially broadening access to programming while preserving meaningful learning outcomes. We investigate its educational value through a month-long online hackathon that welcomed participants from multiple countries, ranging from complete beginners to experienced developers. The hackathon offered three tracks with increasing technical demands. Spark emphasized basic frontend functionality and dynamic features such as buttons, forms, and API calls. Build required backend or database integration. Launch targeted production ready web applications, including deployment. Participants were required to develop projects using only LLM generated code without manual edits and submitted complete chat histories, source code, demo videos, and functionality reports. We assessed educational effectiveness with a mixed methods design that combined standardized project evaluations across functionality, user interface and user experience design, impact, prompt quality, and code readability, along with post-hackathon surveys of perceived learning outcomes and thematic analysis of open-ended feedback. Our findings describe how participants with different backgrounds engage with vibe coding as task complexity increases, how the no manual editing constraint shapes prompting and debugging practices, and what these patterns imply for integrating AI assisted development into programming education and competitive learning environments.

</details>
